# 📓️ S3-CODES-TAX — `warn` → `warning` builder rename, file list (C-3)

Ticket `26/09/30/NON-DESTRUCTIVE-HISTORY-EDITING`. Baseline `HEAD` = `202c4b7b5b1` (auto-commit before the rename). Codemod: `🧪️s3-codes-tax-warning-builders.py` (rules: `MutationMessage::warn` → `MutationMessage::warning`, `MutationOutcome::warn` → `MutationOutcome::warning`, `.warn(` → `.warning(` except embedded `console.warn(`).

- **Pure (1196 files):** applying exactly the codemod rules to the `HEAD` bytes reproduces the working-tree bytes — nothing else changed.

- **Untracked (2 files):** new, not-yet-committed Rust files carrying `warning` builder calls (no `HEAD` baseline; the codemod or their authors spelled them `warning`).
